# Agents · I · part 15

901 entries, investigator to ipc-checker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **investigator**: Read-only code locator — cheapest dispatch on the team. Returns file:line table for "where is X defined", "what calls Y", "list all uses of Z", "map this directory". Output is compressed so the main thread eats ~60% fewer tokens than vanilla Explore. No handoff artifact — the answer dies with the turn. Refuses to suggest fixes; escalate to devcrew:researcher when findings must persist with confidence + risks. ([astragenie/dev-crew](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Read-only diagnosis of failures: CI runs, stack traces, flaky tests, regressions. Classifies a CI failure as code-defect, transient or unknown with evidence. Never fixes. ([Asun28/AIDLC](https://github.com/Asun28/AIDLC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/investigator.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **investigator**: Use PROACTIVELY when user reports problems. Automatically delegate when user mentions bugs, errors, failures, "not working", unexpected behavior, or test failures. Works across web, mobile (Android, iOS, Flutter), and backend issues including Gradle, Xcode, and platform-specific problems. This agent collects evidence, traces root causes, and provides findings for implementation agents to fix. Examples: ([azlekov/my-claude-code](https://github.com/azlekov/my-claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Triages a reported PicoDCC bug — decides whether it is really a defect, reproduces it at the lowest layer that still shows it, finds the root cause, and either files a GitHub issue carrying that evidence or explains why the behaviour is correct. Use when bench testing or JMRI turns up something that looks wrong. Diagnoses only; never fixes. ([bazauto/PicoDCC](https://github.com/bazauto/PicoDCC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Read-only root-cause and impact analyst — traces a reported problem to its proximate and underlying causes, side effects, and regressions, with file:line evidence. Invoked by diagnose's investigate action, or any "why does X happen / what would changing it touch" question. ([BryanBerger98/claude-skills](https://github.com/BryanBerger98/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Public-record investigation leads and timelines ([cantgetalonggta-png/live-online-agent-swarm](https://github.com/cantgetalonggta-png/live-online-agent-swarm/blob/HEAD/permanent/opencode/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Use PROACTIVELY for open-ended codebase exploration that would otherwise pollute the main conversation context — "where is X defined", "which files use Y", "how is Z wired", "find all callers of foo". Returns concise findings with file:line references. Read-only tools; never modifies code. ([Chanmachan/agentic-development-template](https://github.com/Chanmachan/agentic-development-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Pre-flight analysis agent that revalidates WO assumptions, checks dependency completion, analyzes relevant codebase, and flags risks before implementation begins. Dispatched at the start of each WO. ([chieflatif/vibeos-bootstrap](https://github.com/chieflatif/vibeos-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Josheb (the investigator) on the AI team. Investigates and reproduces defects without changing code. Use proactively for errors, failed tests, incidents, unexpected behavior, or before planning a bug fix. ([chrstnjsff/dotfiles](https://github.com/chrstnjsff/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/workspace/.claude/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)

- **Investigator**: Use when: researching codebase structure, tracing code paths, finding all usages of a symbol, investigating bugs, collecting context before implementing, or understanding how a feature works. - **investigator**: Backlog campaign investigator agent. Gathers evidence for router re-route checkpoints via research (external docs/changelog/migration lookup), investigate (root-cause hypothesis loop), and analyze (read-only conventions/architecture/performance evidence gathering) sub-modes; never plans, implements, or mutates queue state. ([CorentinLumineau/blackhole](https://github.com/CorentinLumineau/blackhole/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/investigator.md) · ★1 · 6 copies)
- **investigator**: Hunter-killer debug-and-fix specialist. Read-write. Dispatched when gate:full fails at plan end. Receives a failure cluster (failing test suite / stack trace), investigates root cause, applies a targeted fix, and verifies the fix. Parallelisable — one investigator per failure cluster. Does NOT use the task graph (no tg start/done); reports directly to the orchestrator. ([danalexilewis/taskgraph](https://github.com/danalexilewis/taskgraph/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Read-only investigation specialist. Receives a tactical directive and investigates files, function chains, ASTs, stack traces, architectural patterns, schemas, and API facades. Use when the investigate skill dispatches an investigation task. Returns structured findings only; does not edit or run destructive commands. ([danalexilewis/taskgraph](https://github.com/danalexilewis/taskgraph/blob/HEAD/src/template/.cursor/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: 問いを調べて答えと確かめ方を返す。implementation-flow の `flow:investigation` の調査から呼ぶ。ゴールと問いと終わりの条件を渡すと、コード・ドキュメント・実行結果を確かめて調査結果を返す（項目は本文の「返すもの」）。Issue への記録・実装・ファイルの変更は行わない。 ([DIO0550/design-composer](https://github.com/DIO0550/design-composer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: Cheap read-only fan-out: locate code, trace call paths, answer "where does X happen" — fast, factual, no judgment calls. ([dngioidev/forge](https://github.com/dngioidev/forge/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)

- **investigator**: The squad's incident investigator. A READ-ONLY production-incident investigator that delimits an incident, collects evidence (logs, crashes, analytics, recent deploys, suspect code, feature flags/config), and proposes a ranked root-cause hypothesis WITHOUT changing production. Activate when the user reports a production incident, regression, or anomaly and wants a fast initial diagnosis. Stack-agnostic: reads the project profile at runtime and reaches external systems through whatever observability/analytics/VCS integrations the profile declares. ([gustavobaptista/specops](https://github.com/gustavobaptista/specops/blob/HEAD/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)

- **investigator**: Debugs and reproduces problems - runs the app, streams, tests, or the browser, reads library source, and reports the root cause with evidence. - **investigator**: Read-only deep-dive investigator for the post-build review (spec 027). Dispatched only by the `/jim:review` orchestrator to investigate one assigned changed region or one acceptance criterion in depth — reading surrounding code, tracing callers/consumers, and following data paths the diff alone does not show — then returning structured evidence. Has no write or execute capability by design: a prompt injection embedded in diff or commit content cannot mutate anything because the capability is absent, not merely forbidden. Do not use for capture, planning, or any task that writes files; it neve… ([JamSuite/jim](https://github.com/JamSuite/jim/blob/HEAD/agents/investigator.md) · ★1)
- **investigator**: &gt; **Definition (the law this agent runs under).** Its code is a `runner` (candidate: &gt; `harness/sandbox/investigator/`); this file is its governance half — goal, prompt, gates, &gt; lifecycle. Per [`../../docs/definitions/agent.md`](../../docs/definitions/agent.md): an agent is &gt; an LLM-driven runner an orchestrator dispatches. Owner: `ov`. Lifecycle: **candidate**.
